# Open PTF access (S55): tables

Full 500-seed runs of studies T, U, U10, V, V10, VJ, W, W10 and Y (engine `0c93593`, rows under open PTF access), each compared with the frozen batch's rows on the same seeds (studies A, B, B10, G, G10, H, I and J, engine `ce8d030`: members only; with the switch off the two engines give the same figures). Generated by `python3 dev/tools/s55_open_access.py <compassionism-sim-results checkout> dev/runs/s55-open-access` from BetterToBest/compassionism-sim-results `prog-{t,u,u10,v,v10,vj,w,w10,y}-0c93593` and `prog-{a,b,b10,g,g10,h,i,j}-ce8d030`. Every interval is a 95% paired-bootstrap interval over seeds (10,000 replicates); comparisons between population sizes are independent runs. For the three shares a reduction is a positive number of points, so a positive "open − members" figure means open access helps; for cost and inflation positive means more cost or more inflation. Flags: † interval excludes zero; M also material (1 point; 5% of the members-only row's cost, or of the full design's cost for contrasts; 1 point of the yearly inflation rate); H survives Holm in its preregistered family. Every estimate is also in `estimates.csv`.

- PASS  the share never falls: paS5 <= paS10 <= paS19 (<= paS39), every seed of every row: 0 rows differ
- PASS  every fixed-share row holds its share on every seed and year, below the 40% test only under 40%, above the 55% test only from 60%, no growth tallied (180 rows): 0 differ
- PASS  the growth-off rows (Y, held) never grow (12 rows): 0 differ

- PASS  Shapley values add up to v(all five), as linear maps

### 500 against 10,000 adults (rule 1, last sentence; the programme's rule 3)

0 of 60 open-access decisions differ between 500 and 10,000 adults. 1 of 60 differences pass the scale rule (|Δ| above its Monte Carlo error and the materiality threshold). The programme's rule-1 decision differs between sizes in 0 of 60 outcome-settings.

## 3. Growth: which limit binds, by environment and year (Q28)

The open release row (main values: capacity from the PTFs' own capital, the Hub's cost structure, demand ceiling 49%). paCap, paOps, paDem: share of adult-years from year 1 in zones where that limit stopped growth (%). Share s and capacity c at the year's index (%). "At the ceiling": share of seeds whose s has reached the 49% demand ceiling that year; on every other seed capacity binds that year (operations never binds in the main reading, section 0).

Rule-1 decisions that differ from the main-value row: 175 of 360 (of which 58 cross the materiality line). By setting: demand ceiling 25% 44 (7 material), demand ceiling 75% 14 (12 material), demand ceiling 100% 24 (21 material), no capacity limit 7 (4 material), outside running costs 43 (7 material), growth off (share held at its start) 43 (7 material).

The programme's rule-1 decision (the swept row against no programme) differs from the main-value open row's in 4 outcome-settings: C2.2 design, Reference, 500 adults, 20 years, Wealth<25k y20, demand ceiling 25% (stated + → stated -); C2.2 design, Reference, 500 adults, 20 years, Wealth<25k y20, outside running costs (stated + → stated -); C2.2 design, Reference, 500 adults, 20 years, Wealth<25k y20, growth off (share held at its start) (stated + → stated -); release design, Stress, 500 adults, 20 years, Basic living (BLEI<30, neutral), no capacity limit (stated - → not stated).

## 4. The fixed-share response curve (Q27)

The release row with every zone's PTF share of essential sales held at 10%, ..., 100% (growth off). For each level, the row's effect against no programme and against the members-only release row. Steps: the change across a test minus the mean of the two neighbouring changes (rule 4: a step is claimed where its interval excludes zero and it is at least 1 point, or 5% of the members-only row's cost).

## 5. Synergy under open access (Q25)

The study programme's factorial with its 16 cells that include PTF run under open access (studies U, U10) and the other 15 cells and the none row from B (B10). 137 of 990 contrast decisions (claimable outcomes, 11 contrasts, 18 settings) change between members only and open access;
